1→"""Typed finding/detail accessors for command-layer renderers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Mapping, TypedDict, cast
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→
     9→
    10→class FindingDetail(TypedDict, total=False):
    11→    """Known finding detail keys used by command rendering paths."""
    12→
    13→    category: str
    14→    count: int
    15→    dimension: str
    16→    dimension_name: str
    17→    importers: int
    18→    investigation: bool
    19→    line: int
    20→    lines: list[int]
    21→    strict_score: float
    22→    suggestion: str
    23→
    24→
    25→def state_findings(
    26→    state: state_mod.StateModel | dict[str, Any],
    27→    *,
    28→    strict: bool = False,
    29→) -> dict[str, state_mod.Finding]:
    30→    """Return typed state findings map or raise on contract violations."""
    31→    if strict:
    32→        return state_mod.require_findings(state)
    33→    findings = state.get("findings")
    34→    if isinstance(findings, dict):
    35→        return cast(dict[str, state_mod.Finding], findings)
    36→    return {}
    37→
    38→
    39→def finding_detail(finding: Mapping[str, Any]) -> FindingDetail:
    40→    """Return typed detail mapping for a finding-like payload."""
    41→    detail = finding.get("detail")
    42→    if isinstance(detail, dict):
    43→        return cast(FindingDetail, detail)
    44→    return FindingDetail()
    45→
    46→
    47→def detail_dimension(finding: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    48→    """Return normalized detail.dimension value."""
    49→    return str(finding_detail(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    50→
    51→
    52→def detail_lines(finding: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[int]:
    53→    """Return normalized integer line list from finding detail."""
    54→    raw_lines = finding_detail(finding).get("lines", [])
    55→    if not isinstance(raw_lines, list):
    56→        return []
    57→    return [int(line) for line in raw_lines if isinstance(line, int | float)]
    58→
    59→
    60→def detail_target_line(finding: Mapping[str, Any]) -> int | None:
    61→    """Return primary target line from detail.line or detail.lines[0]."""
    62→    detail = finding_detail(finding)
    63→    line = detail.get("line")
    64→    if isinstance(line, int):
    65→        return line
    66→    lines = detail_lines(finding)
    67→    return lines[0] if lines else None
    68→
    69→
    70→__all__ = [
    71→    "FindingDetail",
    72→    "detail_dimension",
    73→    "detail_lines",
    74→    "detail_target_line",
    75→    "finding_detail",
    76→    "state_findings",
    77→]
    78→

    12→def print_synthesis_guardrail_banner(*, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    13→    """Print synthesis phase guardrail banner with visible load-failure warnings."""
    14→    try:
    15→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    16→        banner = synthesis_phase_banner(resolved_plan)
    17→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    18→        print(
    19→            colorize(
    20→                "  Warning: synthesis guardrail unavailable "
    21→                f"({exc.__class__.__name__}: {exc}).",
    22→                "yellow",
    23→            ),
    24→            file=sys.stderr,
    25→        )
    26→        return
    27→    if banner:
    28→        print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    29→
    30→
    31→__all__ = ["print_synthesis_guardrail_banner"]

1→"""Shared lazy-export helpers for command package ``__init__`` modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, MutableMapping
     7→
     8→
     9→def lazy_module_getattr(
    10→    *,
    11→    name: str,
    12→    module_name: str,
    13→    import_target: str,
    14→    export_names: Iterable[str],
    15→    namespace: MutableMapping[str, object],
    16→) -> object:
    17→    """Lazily import one exported attribute from a sibling command module."""
    18→    allowed = set(export_names)
    19→    if name not in allowed:
    20→        raise AttributeError(f"module {module_name!r} has no attribute {name!r}")
    21→    value = getattr(importlib.import_module(import_target, module_name), name)
    22→    namespace[name] = value
    23→    return value
    24→
    25→
    26→def lazy_module_dir(
    27→    *,
    28→    namespace: Mapping[str, object],
    29→    export_names: Iterable[str],
    30→) -> list[str]:
    31→    """Return module dir() entries including lazy exports."""
    32→    return sorted(set(namespace) | set(export_names))
    33→
    34→

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
    10→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
    11→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→    result = build_work_queue(
    14→        state,
    15→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    16→            status="open",
    17→            count=None,
    18→            plan=plan,
    19→            collapse_clusters=True,
    20→            include_skipped=False,
    21→        ),
    22→    )
    23→    return result["total"]
    24→
    25→
    26→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
    27→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
    28→    if not plan:
    29→        return None
    30→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
    31→
    32→
    33→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
    34→    plan: dict,
    35→    queue_remaining: int,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
    38→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
    39→    strict = scores.get("strict")
    40→    if strict is None:
    41→        return
    42→    print(
    43→        colorize(
    44→            f"\n  Plan-start score: strict {strict:.1f}/100",
    45→            "cyan",
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→    print(
    49→        colorize(
    50→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
    51→            " remaining — complete the queue, then scan to see your updated score.",
    52→            "dim",
    53→        )
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→def print_execution_or_reveal(
    58→    state: dict,
    59→    prev,
    60→    plan: dict | None,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
    63→
    64→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
    65→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
    66→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
    67→    """
    68→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
    69→        try:
    70→            remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    71→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    72→            remaining = 0
    73→        if remaining > 0:
    74→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
    75→            return
    76→
    77→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
    78→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    79→
    80→    print_score_update(state, prev)
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = [
    84→    "get_plan_start_strict",
    85→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
    86→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
    87→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
    88→]
    89→

1→"""Shared queue-construction policy for next/plan command surfaces."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.enums import Status, canonical_status_filter
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     9→
    10→
    11→def build_plan_queue(
    12→    state: dict[str, Any],
    13→    *,
    14→    tier: int | None = None,
    15→    count: int | None = None,
    16→    scope: str | None = None,
    17→    status: str = Status.OPEN.value,
    18→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
    19→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False,
    20→    explain: bool = False,
    21→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    22→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    23→    cluster: str | None = None,
    24→    collapse_clusters: bool = False,
    25→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    26→    """Build one queue with consistent policy defaults used by CLI surfaces."""
    27→    normalized_status = canonical_status_filter(status, default=Status.OPEN.value)
    28→    return build_work_queue(
    29→        state,
    30→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    31→            tier=tier,
    32→            count=count,
    33→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    34→            scope=scope,
    35→            status=normalized_status,
    36→            include_subjective=True,
    37→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
    38→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
    39→            explain=explain,
    40→            plan=plan,
    41→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    42→            cluster=cluster,
    43→            collapse_clusters=collapse_clusters,
    44→        ),
    45→    )
    46→
    47→
    48→__all__ = ["build_plan_queue"]
    49→

1→"""Stable review import mode normalization for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Literal
     6→
     7→ReviewImportMode = Literal[
     8→    "auto",
     9→    "trusted_internal",
    10→    "attested_external",
    11→    "manual_override",
    12→]
    13→
    14→_ALLOWED_MODES: set[str] = {
    15→    "auto",
    16→    "trusted_internal",
    17→    "attested_external",
    18→    "manual_override",
    19→}
    20→
    21→
    22→def normalize_review_import_mode(raw: str | None) -> ReviewImportMode:
    23→    """Normalize user/caller import mode text to one stable mode token."""
    24→    mode = (raw or "auto").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    25→    if mode not in _ALLOWED_MODES:
    26→        allowed = ", ".join(sorted(_ALLOWED_MODES))
    27→        raise ValueError(f"invalid review import mode '{raw}'; expected one of: {allowed}")
    28→    return mode  # type: ignore[return-value]
    29→
    30→
    31→def _validate_mode_compatibility(
    32→    *,
    33→    mode: ReviewImportMode,
    34→    attested: bool,
    35→    manual: bool,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    if mode == "trusted_internal" and (attested or manual):
    38→        raise ValueError(
    39→            "import_mode=trusted_internal cannot be combined with attested/manual flags"
    40→        )
    41→    if mode == "attested_external" and manual:
    42→        raise ValueError(
    43→            "import_mode=attested_external cannot be combined with manual override flags"
    44→        )
    45→    if mode == "manual_override" and attested:
    46→        raise ValueError(
    47→            "import_mode=manual_override cannot be combined with attested external flags"
    48→        )
    49→
    50→
    51→def _apply_trusted_internal_defaults(
    52→    trusted_source: bool,
    53→    trusted_label: str | None,
    54→) -> tuple[bool, str, bool, bool]:
    55→    if not trusted_source:
    56→        trusted_source = True
    57→    if not isinstance(trusted_label, str) or not trusted_label.strip():
    58→        trusted_label = "trusted internal run-batches import"
    59→    return trusted_source, trusted_label, False, False
    60→
    61→
    62→def apply_review_import_mode(
    63→    *,
    64→    import_mode: str | None,
    65→    trusted_assessment_source: bool,
    66→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None,
    67→    attested_external: bool,
    68→    manual_override: bool,
    69→) -> tuple[bool, str | None, bool, bool]:
    70→    """Apply a canonical import mode over legacy booleans without dropping compatibility."""
    71→    mode = normalize_review_import_mode(import_mode)
    72→    trusted_source = bool(trusted_assessment_source)
    73→    trusted_label = trusted_assessment_label
    74→    attested = bool(attested_external)
    75→    manual = bool(manual_override)
    76→
    77→    _validate_mode_compatibility(mode=mode, attested=attested, manual=manual)
    78→
    79→    if mode == "trusted_internal":
    80→        return _apply_trusted_internal_defaults(trusted_source, trusted_label)
    81→    if mode == "attested_external":
    82→        return trusted_source, trusted_label, True, False
    83→    if mode == "manual_override":
    84→        return trusted_source, trusted_label, False, True
    85→
    86→    return trusted_source, trusted_label, attested, manual
    87→

    89→__all__ = [
    90→    "ReviewImportMode",
    91→    "apply_review_import_mode",
    92→    "normalize_review_import_mode",

1→"""Shared review packet preparation helpers used by prepare/batch flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence import review as review_mod
     9→
    10→from .constants import DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES
    11→
    12→
    13→def redacted_review_config(config: dict[str, object] | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    14→    """Return review config payload with score-target hints removed."""
    15→    if not isinstance(config, dict):
    16→        return {}
    17→    return {key: value for key, value in config.items() if key != "target_strict_score"}
    18→
    19→
    20→def coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config: dict[str, object] | None) -> int | None:
    21→    """Resolve per-batch review file cap from config (0/negative => unlimited)."""
    22→    raw = (config or {}).get("review_batch_max_files", DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES)
    23→    try:
    24→        value = int(raw)
    25→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    26→        return DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES
    27→    return value if value > 0 else None
    28→
    29→
    30→def build_run_batches_next_command(
    31→    *,
    32→    include_scan_after_import: bool,
    33→    retrospective: bool,
    34→    retrospective_max_issues: int,
    35→    retrospective_max_batch_items: int,
    36→) -> str:
    37→    """Build canonical follow-up command for batch execution."""
    38→    command = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel"
    39→    if include_scan_after_import:
    40→        command += " --scan-after-import"
    41→    if retrospective:
    42→        command += (
    43→            " --retrospective"
    44→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
    45→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
    46→        )
    47→    return command
    48→
    49→
    50→def build_review_packet(
    51→    *,
    52→    path: Path,
    53→    lang_run,
    54→    state: dict[str, Any],
    55→    config: dict[str, object] | None,
    56→    dimensions: list[str] | None,
    57→    files: list[str] | None,
    58→    narrative: dict[str, Any],
    59→    retrospective: bool,
    60→    retrospective_max_issues: int,
    61→    retrospective_max_batch_items: int,
    62→    include_scan_after_import: bool,
    63→    prepare_holistic_review_fn=None,
    64→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    65→    """Create the canonical holistic review packet payload."""
    66→    prepare_fn = prepare_holistic_review_fn or review_mod.prepare_holistic_review
    67→    packet = prepare_fn(
    68→        path,
    69→        lang_run,
    70→        state,
    71→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    72→            dimensions=dimensions,
    73→            files=files or None,
    74→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    75→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
    76→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    77→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    78→        ),
    79→    )
    80→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
    81→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
    82→    packet["next_command"] = build_run_batches_next_command(
    83→        include_scan_after_import=include_scan_after_import,
    84→        retrospective=retrospective,
    85→        retrospective_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
    86→        retrospective_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
    87→    )
    88→    return packet
    89→
    90→
    91→__all__ = [
    92→    "build_review_packet",
    93→    "build_run_batches_next_command",
    94→    "coerce_review_batch_file_limit",
    95→    "redacted_review_config",
    96→]
    97→

1→"""Shared subjective-assessment integrity helpers for review import flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→
     8→def subjective_at_target_dimensions(
     9→    state_or_dim_scores: dict,
    10→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    11→    *,
    12→    target: float,
    13→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
    14→    matches_target_score_fn,
    15→) -> list[dict]:
    16→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective rows that sit on the target threshold."""
    17→    state = state_or_dim_scores
    18→    if dim_scores is None:
    19→        dim_scores = state_or_dim_scores
    20→        state = {"dimension_scores": dim_scores}
    21→
    22→    rows: list[dict] = []
    23→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    24→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    25→            continue
    26→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    27→        if matches_target_score_fn(strict_val, target):
    28→            rows.append(
    29→                {
    30→                    "name": str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    31→                    "score": strict_val,
    32→                    "cli_keys": list(entry.get("cli_keys", [])),
    33→                }
    34→            )
    35→    rows.sort(key=lambda item: item["name"].lower())
    36→    return rows
    37→
    38→
    39→def bind_scorecard_subjective_at_target(
    40→    *,
    41→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn=None,
    42→    reporting_dimensions_mod=None,
    43→    subjective_integrity_mod,
    44→) -> Callable[..., list[dict]]:
    45→    """Bind scorecard-specific dependencies once for import/output call sites.
    46→
    47→    ``reporting_dimensions_mod`` is kept for compatibility; new callers should
    48→    pass ``scorecard_subjective_entries_fn`` explicitly.
    49→    """
    50→    if scorecard_subjective_entries_fn is None and reporting_dimensions_mod is not None:
    51→        scorecard_subjective_entries_fn = (
    52→            reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries
    53→        )
    54→    if not callable(scorecard_subjective_entries_fn):
    55→        raise ValueError("scorecard_subjective_entries_fn must be callable")
    56→
    57→    def bound(
    58→        state_or_dim_scores: dict[str, object],
    59→        dim_scores: dict[str, object] | None = None,
    60→        *,
    61→        target: float,
    62→    ) -> list[dict]:
    63→        return subjective_at_target_dimensions(
    64→            state_or_dim_scores,
    65→            dim_scores,
    66→            target=target,
    67→            scorecard_subjective_entries_fn=scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
    68→            matches_target_score_fn=subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score,
    69→        )
    70→
    71→    return bound
    72→
    73→
    74→__all__ = [
    75→    "bind_scorecard_subjective_at_target",
    76→    "subjective_at_target_dimensions",
    77→]
    78→

     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.review.runtime.setup import (
     6→    setup_lang,

     9→
    10→_LAZY_EXPORTS = {
    11→    "runner_helpers": "desloppify.app.commands.review.runner_helpers",
    12→    "policy": ".policy",
    13→}
    14→
    15→__all__ = ["setup_lang", "setup_lang_concrete", *_LAZY_EXPORTS]
    16→
    17→
    18→def __getattr__(name: str):
    19→    target = _LAZY_EXPORTS.get(name)
    20→    if target is None:
    21→        raise AttributeError(f"module {__name__!r} has no attribute {name!r}")
    22→    if target.startswith("."):
    23→        value = importlib.import_module(target, __name__)
    24→    else:
    25→        value = importlib.import_module(target)
    26→    globals()[name] = value
    27→    return value
    28→
    29→
    30→def __dir__() -> list[str]:
    31→    return sorted(set(globals()) | set(__all__))
    32→

1→"""Centralized execution policy for review batch orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import math
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import (
     9→    coerce_non_negative_float as _coerce_non_negative_float,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import (
    12→    coerce_non_negative_int as _coerce_non_negative_int,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import (
    15→    coerce_positive_float as _coerce_positive_float,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import (
    18→    coerce_positive_int as _coerce_positive_int,
    19→)
    20→
    21→from ..orchestration.runtime_defaults import DEFAULT_BATCH_STALL_KILL_SECONDS
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25→class BatchRunPolicy:
    26→    """Resolved runtime knobs for `review --run-batches` execution."""
    27→
    28→    run_parallel: bool
    29→    max_parallel_batches: int
    30→    heartbeat_seconds: float
    31→    batch_timeout_seconds: int
    32→    batch_max_retries: int
    33→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds: float
    34→    stall_warning_seconds: int
    35→    stall_kill_seconds: int
    36→
    37→    def max_parallel_workers(self, total_batches: int) -> int:
    38→        if total_batches <= 0:
    39→            return 1
    40→        if self.run_parallel:
    41→            return min(total_batches, self.max_parallel_batches)
    42→        return 1
    43→
    44→    def worst_case_minutes(self, total_batches: int) -> int:
    45→        waves = math.ceil(total_batches / self.max_parallel_workers(total_batches))
    46→        return max(1, math.ceil((waves * self.batch_timeout_seconds) / 60))
    47→
    48→
    49→def resolve_batch_run_policy(args) -> BatchRunPolicy:
    50→    """Build one canonical policy object from CLI args."""
    51→    return BatchRunPolicy(
    52→        run_parallel=bool(getattr(args, "parallel", False)),
    53→        max_parallel_batches=_coerce_positive_int(
    54→            getattr(args, "max_parallel_batches", None),
    55→            default=3,
    56→            minimum=1,
    57→        ),
    58→        heartbeat_seconds=_coerce_positive_float(
    59→            getattr(args, "batch_heartbeat_seconds", None),
    60→            default=15.0,
    61→            minimum=0.1,
    62→        ),
    63→        batch_timeout_seconds=_coerce_positive_int(
    64→            getattr(args, "batch_timeout_seconds", None),
    65→            default=2 * 60 * 60,
    66→            minimum=1,
    67→        ),
    68→        batch_max_retries=_coerce_positive_int(
    69→            getattr(args, "batch_max_retries", None),
    70→            default=1,
    71→            minimum=0,
    72→        ),
    73→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=_coerce_non_negative_float(
    74→            getattr(args, "batch_retry_backoff_seconds", None),
    75→            default=2.0,
    76→        ),
    77→        stall_warning_seconds=_coerce_non_negative_int(
    78→            getattr(args, "batch_stall_warning_seconds", None),
    79→            default=0,
    80→        ),
    81→        stall_kill_seconds=_coerce_non_negative_int(
    82→            getattr(args, "batch_stall_kill_seconds", None),
    83→            default=DEFAULT_BATCH_STALL_KILL_SECONDS,
    84→        ),
    85→    )
    86→
    87→
    88→__all__ = ["BatchRunPolicy", "resolve_batch_run_policy"]
    89→

@@ -159,3 +134,45 @@ def _cmd_fix_review(args):

+def _cmd_fix_review(args):
+    """Prepare structured review data with dimension templates for AI evaluation."""
+    runtime = command_runtime(args)
+    lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
+    if not lang_cfg:
+        print_error("could not detect language. Use --lang.")
+        sys.exit(1)
+
+    state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
+    path = Path(args.path)
+
+    lang_run, found_files = review_runtime_mod.setup_lang_concrete(
+        lang_cfg,
+        path,
+        runtime.config,
+    )
+    data = review_mod.prepare_review(
+        path,
+        lang_run,
+        state,
+        options=review_mod.ReviewPrepareOptions(files=found_files or None),
+    )
+
+    if data["total_candidates"] == 0:
+        print(
+            colorize(
+                "\n  All production files have been reviewed. Nothing to do.", "green"
+            )
+        )
+        _print_subjective_integrity_refresh_hints(state)
+        return
+
+    print(
+        colorize(f"\n  {data['total_candidates']} files need design review\n", "bold")
+    )
+    _print_dimension_prompts(data)
+    _print_lang_guide(data, lang_run=lang_run)
+
+    write_query(data)
+    _print_review_agent_plan()

1→"""Runtime setup helpers for review command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.pathing.api import rel
    10→from desloppify.core.io.api import log
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import FileZoneMap
    12→from desloppify.languages import runtime as lang_runtime_mod
    13→
    14→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    15→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import LangConfig
    16→    from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun
    17→
    18→
    19→def setup_lang(
    20→    lang: LangConfig,
    21→    path: Path,
    22→    config: dict[str, Any],
    23→    *,
    24→    make_lang_run_fn: Callable[[LangConfig], LangRun],
    25→    file_zone_map_cls: type[FileZoneMap],
    26→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str],
    27→    log_fn: Callable[[str], None],
    28→) -> tuple[LangRun, list[str]]:
    29→    """Build LangRun with zone map + dep graph and return (run, files)."""
    30→    lang_run = make_lang_run_fn(lang)
    31→    files: list[str] = []
    32→
    33→    if lang_run.zone_rules and lang_run.file_finder:
    34→        files = lang_run.file_finder(path)
    35→        zone_overrides = config.get("zone_overrides") or None
    36→        lang_run.zone_map = file_zone_map_cls(
    37→            files, lang_run.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel_fn, overrides=zone_overrides
    38→        )
    39→
    40→    if lang_run.build_dep_graph and lang_run.dep_graph is None:
    41→        try:
    42→            lang_run.dep_graph = lang_run.build_dep_graph(path)
    43→        except (
    44→            OSError,
    45→            UnicodeDecodeError,
    46→            ValueError,
    47→            TypeError,
    48→            RuntimeError,
    49→        ) as exc:
    50→            log_fn(f"  dep graph unavailable for review context: {exc}")
    51→
    52→    return lang_run, files
    53→
    54→
    55→def setup_lang_concrete(lang: LangConfig, path: Path, config: dict) -> tuple[LangRun, list[str]]:
    56→    """Build LangRun with zone map + dep graph using concrete dependencies."""
    57→    return setup_lang(
    58→        lang,
    59→        path,
    60→        config,
    61→        make_lang_run_fn=lang_runtime_mod.make_lang_run,
    62→        file_zone_map_cls=FileZoneMap,
    63→        rel_fn=rel,
    64→        log_fn=log,
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→__all__ = ["setup_lang", "setup_lang_concrete"]
    69→

1→"""Shared review-workflow command text used by review/fix flows."""
     2→

     4→

     7→)
     8→CLAUDE_EXTERNAL_START_COMMAND = (
     9→    "desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude"
    10→)
    11→
    12→
    13→def normalized_next_command(next_command: str | None) -> str:
    14→    """Return one normalized subjective follow-up command string."""
    15→    text = str(next_command or "").strip()
    16→    return text or DEFAULT_REVIEW_NEXT_COMMAND
    17→
    18→
    19→def next_subjective_command_text(next_command: str | None) -> str:
    20→    """Render one shared 'next subjective command' guidance line."""
    21→    return (
    22→        "Next command to improve subjective scores: "
    23→        f"`{normalized_next_command(next_command)}`"
    24→    )
    25→
    26→
    27→def claude_durable_path_text(*, submit_command_hint: str = "--external-submit") -> str:
    28→    """Render one shared Claude durable-path guidance line."""
    29→    return (
    30→        "Claude cloud durable path: "
    31→        f"`{CLAUDE_EXTERNAL_START_COMMAND}` then run the printed "
    32→        f"`{submit_command_hint}` command"
    33→    )
    34→
    35→
    36→def findings_only_fallback_text(
    37→    *,
    38→    include_prepare_step: bool = False,
    39→    include_scan_after_import: bool = False,
    40→) -> str:
    41→    """Render one shared findings-only fallback guidance line."""
    42→    import_cmd = "desloppify review --import findings.json"
    43→    if include_scan_after_import:
    44→        import_cmd = f"{import_cmd} && desloppify scan"
    45→    if include_prepare_step:
    46→        return (
    47→            "Findings-only fallback: `desloppify review --prepare`, then "
    48→            f"`{import_cmd}`"
    49→        )
    50→    return f"Findings-only fallback: `{import_cmd}`"
    51→
    52→
    53→def prepare_agent_plan_lines(next_command: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    54→    """Return shared plan lines for `review --prepare` output."""
    55→    return (
    56→        f"1. Preferred: `{normalized_next_command(next_command)}`",
    57→        "2. Cloud/manual fallback: run external reviewers, merge to findings.json, then import",
    58→        f"3. {claude_durable_path_text()}",
    59→        f"4. {findings_only_fallback_text()}",

    71→        "5. " + claude_durable_path_text(submit_command_hint="--external-submit ... --scan-after-import"),

1→"""Agent-environment detection helpers for scan reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→
     7→
     8→def is_agent_environment() -> bool:
     9→    """Return True when scan is running inside a supported AI agent runtime."""
    10→    return bool(
    11→        os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE")
    12→        or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_AGENT")
    13→        or os.environ.get("GEMINI_CLI")
    14→        or os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX_NETWORK_DISABLED")
    15→        or os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX")
    16→        or os.environ.get("CURSOR_TRACE_ID")
    17→    )
    18→
    19→
    20→def detect_agent_interface() -> str | None:
    21→    """Resolve current interface key used by `update-skill`."""
    22→    if os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE"):
    23→        return "claude"
    24→    if os.environ.get("GEMINI_CLI"):
    25→        return "gemini"
    26→    if os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX_NETWORK_DISABLED") or os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX"):
    27→        return "codex"
    28→    if os.environ.get("CURSOR_TRACE_ID"):
    29→        return "cursor"
    30→    return None
    31→
    32→
    33→__all__ = ["detect_agent_interface", "is_agent_environment"]
    34→

1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    10→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    13→
    14→
    15→def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    16→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    17→    # CI profile always passes
    18→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    19→        return
    20→
    21→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    22→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    23→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    24→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    25→            print(
    26→                colorize(
    27→                    '  --force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    28→                    "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    29→                    'completion"',
    30→                    "red",
    31→                ),
    32→                file=sys.stderr,
    33→            )
    34→            sys.exit(1)
    35→        print(
    36→            colorize(
    37→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    38→                "Plan-start score will be reset.",
    39→                "yellow",
    40→            )
    41→        )
    42→        # Clear plan_start_scores
    43→        try:
    44→            plan = load_plan()
    45→            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    46→                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    47→                save_plan(plan)
    48→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    49→            _ = exc
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    53→    try:
    54→        plan = load_plan()
    55→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    56→        return
    57→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    58→        return  # No active cycle
    59→
    60→    # Count plan-aware remaining items
    61→    try:
    62→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    63→        remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    64→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    65→        return
    66→    if remaining == 0:
    67→        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    68→
    69→    # GATE
    70→    print(
    71→        colorize(
    72→            f"\n  WARNING: {remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
    73→            " remaining in your queue.",
    74→            "red",
    75→        ),
    76→        file=sys.stderr,
    77→    )
    78→    print(
    79→        colorize(
    80→            "  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.",
    81→            "red",
    82→        ),
    83→        file=sys.stderr,
    84→    )
    85→    print(
    86→        colorize(
    87→            "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.",
    88→            "dim",
    89→        ),
    90→        file=sys.stderr,
    91→    )
    92→    print(
    93→        colorize(
    94→            "\n  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):",
    95→            "dim",
    96→        ),
    97→        file=sys.stderr,
    98→    )
    99→    print(
   100→        colorize(
   101→            '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
   102→            "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
   103→            'completion"',
   104→            "yellow",
   105→        ),
   106→        file=sys.stderr,
   107→    )
   108→    sys.exit(1)
   109→
   110→
   111→__all__ = ["scan_queue_preflight"]
   112→

1→"""Skill auto-install/update helpers for scan workflows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands import update_skill as update_skill_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_agent_env import (
     9→    detect_agent_interface,
    10→    is_agent_environment,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.core import skill_docs as skill_docs_mod
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _try_auto_update_skill() -> None:
    18→    """Attempt to auto-install or update the skill document best-effort."""
    19→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
    20→    if install and not install.stale:
    21→        return
    22→
    23→    try:
    24→        interface = (
    25→            update_skill_mod.resolve_interface(install=install)
    26→            if install
    27→            else detect_agent_interface()
    28→        )
    29→        if interface:
    30→            update_skill_mod.update_installed_skill(interface)
    31→    except (ImportError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
    32→        logger.debug("Skill auto-update skipped: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
    33→        return
    34→
    35→
    36→def auto_update_skill() -> None:
    37→    """Auto-install or update the skill document if an agent runtime is detected."""
    38→    if not is_agent_environment():
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    _try_auto_update_skill()
    42→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
    43→    if not install:
    44→        names = ", ".join(sorted(skill_docs_mod.SKILL_TARGETS))
    45→        print(
    46→            f"No skill document found. Install one for better workflow guidance: "
    47→            f"desloppify update-skill <{names}>"
    48→        )
    49→    elif install.stale:
    50→        print(
    51→            f"Skill document is outdated "
    52→            f"(v{install.version}, current v{skill_docs_mod.SKILL_VERSION}). "
    53→            f"Run: desloppify update-skill"
    54→        )
    55→
    56→
    57→__all__ = ["auto_update_skill"]
    58→

tool_result Read
```
1→"""Shared dynamic path proxy for runtime-root-aware path exports."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→
     9→class DynamicPathProxy:
    10→    """Path-like proxy that resolves its underlying path on each access."""
    11→
    12→    def __init__(self, resolver: Callable[[], Path], *, label: str) -> None:
    13→        self._resolver = resolver
    14→        self._label = label
    15→
    16→    def _value(self) -> Path:
    17→        return self._resolver()
    18→
    19→    def __fspath__(self) -> str:
    20→        return str(self._value())
    21→
    22→    def __str__(self) -> str:
    23→        return str(self._value())
    24→
    25→    def __repr__(self) -> str:
    26→        return f"{self._label}({self._value()!s})"
    27→
    28→    def __truediv__(self, other: object) -> Path:
    29→        return self._value() / other
    30→
    31→    def __rtruediv__(self, other: object) -> Path:
    32→        return Path(other) / self._value()
    33→
    34→    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> object:
    35→        return getattr(self._value(), name)
    36→
    37→
    38→__all__ = ["DynamicPathProxy"]
    39→
```

 """Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
 
-Blocks --prepare / --run-batches / --external-start when the objective plan
-still has open items, unless --force-review-rerun is set.  Also auto-clears
-stale subjective markers before a new review cycle.
+Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective findings and/or subjective queue
+work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
+markers before a new cycle.
 """
 
 from __future__ import annotations
 
+import re
 import sys
 
 from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
@@ -56,6 +57,143 @@ def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
     return sorted(scored)
 
 
+def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
+    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
+    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
+    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
+
+
+def _count_open_subjective_queue_items(
+    state: StateModel,
+    *,
+    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
+) -> int:
+    """Count open synthetic subjective queue items for rerun gating."""
+    normalized_dims = (
+        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
+        if dimensions is not None
+        else None
+    )
+    queue = build_work_queue(
+        state,
+        options=QueueBuildOptions(
+            status="open",
+            include_subjective=True,
+            count=None,
+        ),
+    )
+    count = 0
+    for item in queue.get("items", []):
+        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension":
+            continue
+        if normalized_dims is None:
+            count += 1
+            continue
+        detail = item.get("detail")
+        raw_dim = detail.get("dimension", "") if isinstance(detail, dict) else ""
+        item_dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
+        if item_dim and item_dim in normalized_dims:
+            count += 1
+    return count
+
+
+def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
+    state: StateModel,
+    *,
+    dimensions: set[str] | None,
+    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
+) -> list[str]:
+    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
+    if dimensions is None:
+        return scored_dims
+    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
+    return sorted(
+        dim
+        for dim in scored_dims
+        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
+    )
+
+
+def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
+    state: StateModel,
+    *,
+    blocking_dims: list[str],
+) -> tuple[int, int]:
+    objective_result = build_work_queue(
+        state,
+        options=QueueBuildOptions(
+            status="open",
+            include_subjective=False,
+            count=None,
+        ),
+    )
+    objective_total = objective_result["total"]
+    subjective_total = _count_open_subjective_queue_items(
+        state,
+        dimensions=set(blocking_dims),
+    )
+    return objective_total, subjective_total
+
+
+def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
+    *,
+    blocking_dims: list[str],
+    objective_total: int,
+    subjective_total: int,
+    dimensions: set[str] | None,
+) -> None:
+    print(
+        colorize(
+            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
+            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
+            "red",
+        ),
+        file=sys.stderr,
+    )
+    print(
+        colorize(
+            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
+            "yellow",
+        ),
+        file=sys.stderr,
+    )
+    if objective_total > 0:
+        print(
+            colorize(
+                f"  Open objective finding(s): {objective_total}",
+                "yellow",
+            ),
+            file=sys.stderr,
+        )
+    if subjective_total > 0:
+        print(
+            colorize(
+                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
+                "yellow",
+            ),
+            file=sys.stderr,
+        )
+    print("", file=sys.stderr)
+    if dimensions is not None:
+        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
+        if unscored:
+            print(
+                colorize(
+                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
+                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
+                    "dim",
+                ),
+                file=sys.stderr,
+            )
+    print(
+        colorize(
+            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
+            "dim",
+        ),
+        file=sys.stderr,
+    )
+
+
 def review_rerun_preflight(
     state: StateModel,
     args,
@@ -65,73 +203,46 @@ def review_rerun_preflight(

+def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:
+    """Best-effort clear for config.needs_rescan after a successful scan merge."""
+    if not config.get("needs_rescan"):
+        return
+    try:
+        config["needs_rescan"] = False
+        _save_config(config)
+    except OSError as exc:
+        _ = exc
+        config["needs_rescan"] = True
+
+
+def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
+    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
+    return bool(
+        plan.get("queue_order")
+        or plan.get("overrides")
+        or plan.get("clusters")
+        or plan.get("skipped")
+    )
+
+
+def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
+    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
+    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
+        return False
+    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
+    if recon.resurfaced:
+        print(
+            colorize(
+                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
+                "cyan",
+            )
+        )
+    return bool(recon.changes)
+
+
+def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
+    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
+    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
+    if sync.injected:
+        print(
+            colorize(
+                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
+                "cyan",
+            )
+        )
+    return bool(sync.changes)
+
+
+def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
+    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
+    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
+    if sync.pruned:
+        print(
+            colorize(
+                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
+                "cyan",
+            )
+        )
+    if sync.injected:
+        print(
+            colorize(
+                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} stale subjective dimension(s) queued for refresh.",
+                "cyan",
+            )
+        )
+    return bool(sync.changes)
+
+
+def _sync_auto_clusters(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
+    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
+    return bool(auto_cluster_findings(plan, state))
+
+
+def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
+    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
+    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
+        return False
+    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
+    if scores.strict is None:
+        return False
+    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
+        "strict": scores.strict,
+        "overall": scores.overall,
+        "objective": scores.objective,
+        "verified": scores.verified,
+    }
+    return True
+
+
+def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
+    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
+) -> bool:
+    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
+    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
+        return False
+
+    try:
+        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
+
+        queue_empty = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan) == 0
+    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
+        _ = exc
+        return False
+    if not queue_empty:
+        return False
+    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
+    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
+    return True
+
+
+def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
+    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
+    try:
+        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
+        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
+        dirty = False
+
+        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
+            dirty = True
+        if _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions):

+        if _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_stale_dimensions):

+        if _sync_auto_clusters(plan, runtime.state):

+        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
+        if seeded:

+        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
+        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
+        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
+            dirty = True
+
+        if dirty:
+            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
+    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
+        _ = exc

1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing synthesis-clusters rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→import re
    13→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    17→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    18→    VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS,
    19→    Cluster,
    20→    PlanModel,
    21→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    22→    synthesis_clusters,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    25→    SYNTHESIS_ID,
    26→    review_finding_snapshot_hash,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→# Finding-ID citation extraction
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→FINDING_ID_RE = re.compile(r"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}")
    38→
    39→
    40→def extract_finding_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    41→    """Extract finding IDs cited in free text.
    42→
    43→    Matches full finding IDs (e.g. ``review::abcdef12``) or bare 8+ char
    44→    hex suffixes that correspond to a known finding.
    45→    """
    46→    cited: set[str] = set()
    47→    # Match full finding IDs
    48→    for match in FINDING_ID_RE.finditer(text):
    49→        candidate = match.group()
    50→        if candidate in valid_ids:
    51→            cited.add(candidate)
    52→    # Match 8+ char hex suffixes
    53→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", text):
    54→        for valid_id in valid_ids:
    55→            if valid_id.endswith("::" + token):
    56→                cited.add(valid_id)
    57→                break
    58→    return cited
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# Completed-work helpers
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    66→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    67→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    68→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    69→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    70→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    71→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    72→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    73→                if ts:
    74→                    return str(ts)
    75→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    76→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    77→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    78→    return None
    79→
    80→
    81→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    82→    open_findings: dict[str, dict],
    83→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict],
    84→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    85→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open findings.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    88→    A dimension with both resolved and open findings signals a potential
    89→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    90→    """
    91→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    92→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    93→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    94→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    95→        return ""
    96→
    97→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    98→    for fid, f in open_findings.items():
    99→        dim = _dimension(f)
   100→        if dim:
   101→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   102→
   103→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   104→    for fid, f in resolved_findings.items():
   105→        dim = _dimension(f)
   106→        if dim:
   107→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   108→
   109→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
   110→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
   111→        recurring[dim] = {
   112→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
   113→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
   114→        }
   115→    return recurring
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Data types
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→@dataclass
   123→class SynthesisInput:
   124→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
   125→
   126→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
   127→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
   128→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current synthesis-clusters to update
   129→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
   130→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
   131→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
   132→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
   133→    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
   134→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict]   # full finding objects for resolved IDs
   135→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last synthesis
   136→
   137→
   138→@dataclass
   139→class DismissedFinding:
   140→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
   141→
   142→    finding_id: str
   143→    reason: str
   144→
   145→
   146→@dataclass
   147→class ContradictionNote:
   148→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
   149→
   150→    kept: str
   151→    dismissed: str
   152→    reason: str
   153→
   154→
   155→@dataclass
   156→class SynthesisResult:
   157→    """Parsed and validated LLM synthesis output."""
   158→
   159→    strategy_summary: str
   160→    epics: list[dict]
   161→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
   162→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
   163→    priority_rationale: str = ""
   164→
   165→
   166→@dataclass
   167→class SynthesisMutationResult:
   168→    """What changed when synthesis was applied to the plan."""
   169→
   170→    epics_created: int = 0
   171→    epics_updated: int = 0
   172→    epics_completed: int = 0
   173→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
   174→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
   175→    strategy_summary: str = ""
   176→    synthesis_version: int = 0
   177→    dry_run: bool = False
   178→
   179→
   180→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→# Input collection
   182→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→
   184→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
   185→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
   186→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   187→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   188→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   189→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   190→
   191→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   192→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   193→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   194→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   195→            continue
   196→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   197→            open_review[fid] = f
   198→        else:
   199→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   200→
   201→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   202→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   203→    new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
   204→    resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids
   205→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   206→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   207→
   208→    # Resolved finding objects (for REFLECT stage)
   209→    resolved_finding_objs = {
   210→        fid: findings[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in findings
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    # Completed clusters since last synthesis completion
   214→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   215→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   216→    if last_completed:
   217→        recent_completed = [
   218→            c for c in all_completed
   219→            if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   220→        ]
   221→    else:
   222→        recent_completed = list(all_completed)
   223→
   224→    return SynthesisInput(
   225→        open_findings=open_review,
   226→        mechanical_findings=open_mechanical,
   227→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   228→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   229→        new_since_last=new_since,
   230→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   231→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   232→        synthesis_version=version,
   233→        resolved_findings=resolved_finding_objs,
   234→        completed_clusters=recent_completed,
   235→    )
   236→
   237→
   238→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   239→# Prompt construction
   240→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   241→
   242→_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   243→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   244→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.
   245→
   246→Your plan should:
   247→- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   248→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   249→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   250→- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   251→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   252→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   253→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently
   254→
   255→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   256→
   257→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   258→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   259→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   260→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   261→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   262→- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   263→- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   264→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   265→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   266→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings
   267→
   268→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
   269→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
   270→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   271→
   272→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   273→{
   274→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   275→  "epics": [
   276→    {
   277→      "name": "slug-name",
   278→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   279→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   280→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   281→      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   282→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   283→      "agent_safe": true,
   284→      "dependency_order": 1,
   285→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   286→      "status": "pending"
   287→    }
   288→  ],
   289→  "dismissed_findings": [
   290→    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}
   291→  ],
   292→  "contradiction_notes": [
   293→    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
   294→  ],
   295→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   296→}
   297→"""
   298→
   299→
   300→def build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str:
   301→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all finding data."""
   302→    parts: list[str] = []
   303→
   304→    # Section: existing epics
   305→    if si.existing_epics:
   306→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   307→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   308→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   309→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   310→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   311→            fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   312→            parts.append(
   313→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   314→                f"\n  Findings: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   315→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   316→            )
   317→        parts.append("")
   318→
   319→    # Section: what changed
   320→    if si.new_since_last:
   321→        parts.append(f"## New findings since last synthesis ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   322→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   323→            f = si.open_findings.get(fid, {})
   324→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   325→        parts.append("")
   326→
   327→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   328→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last synthesis ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   329→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   330→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   331→        parts.append("")
   332→
   333→    # Section: all open review findings
   334→    parts.append(f"## All open review findings ({len(si.open_findings)})")
   335→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_findings.items()):
   336→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   337→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   338→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   339→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   340→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   341→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   342→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   343→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   344→        if dimension:
   345→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   346→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   347→        if suggestion:
   348→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   349→    parts.append("")
   350→
   351→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   352→    if si.dimension_scores:
   353→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   354→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   355→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   356→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   357→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   358→                issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   359→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   360→        parts.append("")
   361→
   362→    # Section: previously dismissed
   363→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   364→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   365→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   366→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   367→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   368→        parts.append("")
   369→
   370→    return "\n".join(parts)
   371→
   372→
   373→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   374→# Result parsing
   375→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   376→
   377→def parse_synthesis_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> SynthesisResult:
   378→    """Parse and validate raw LLM output into a SynthesisResult.
   379→
   380→    Invalid finding IDs are silently dropped from epics and dismissals.
   381→    """
   382→    strategy_summary = str(raw.get("strategy_summary", ""))
   383→
   384→    epics: list[dict] = []
   385→    for raw_epic in raw.get("epics", []):
   386→        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):
   387→            continue
   388→        name = str(raw_epic.get("name", "")).strip()
   389→        if not name:
   390→            continue
   391→        # Validate direction
   392→        direction = str(raw_epic.get("direction", "simplify")).strip()
   393→        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:
   394→            direction = "simplify"
   395→        # Filter to valid finding IDs
   396→        finding_ids = [
   397→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   398→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   399→        ]
   400→        dismissed = [
   401→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("dismissed", [])
   402→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   403→        ]
   404→        action_steps = [
   405→            str(s) for s in raw_epic.get("action_steps", [])
   406→            if isinstance(s, str)
   407→        ]
   408→
   409→        epics.append({
   410→            "name": name,
   411→            "thesis": str(raw_epic.get("thesis", "")),
   412→            "direction": direction,
   413→            "root_cause": str(raw_epic.get("root_cause", "")),
   414→            "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   415→            "dismissed": dismissed,
   416→            "agent_safe": bool(raw_epic.get("agent_safe", False)),
   417→            "dependency_order": int(raw_epic.get("dependency_order", 999)),
   418→            "action_steps": action_steps,
   419→            "status": str(raw_epic.get("status", "pending")),
   420→        })
   421→
   422→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = []
   423→    for d in raw.get("dismissed_findings", []):
   424→        if not isinstance(d, dict):
   425→            continue
   426→        fid = str(d.get("finding_id", ""))
   427→        if fid in valid_ids:
   428→            dismissed_findings.append(
   429→                DismissedFinding(finding_id=fid, reason=str(d.get("reason", "")))
   430→            )
   431→
   432→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []
   433→    for c in raw.get("contradiction_notes", []):
   434→        if not isinstance(c, dict):
   435→            continue
   436→        contradiction_notes.append(ContradictionNote(
   437→            kept=str(c.get("kept", "")),
   438→            dismissed=str(c.get("dismissed", "")),
   439→            reason=str(c.get("reason", "")),
   440→        ))
   441→
   442→    priority_rationale = str(raw.get("priority_rationale", ""))
   443→
   444→    return SynthesisResult(
   445→        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,
   446→        epics=epics,
   447→        dismissed_findings=dismissed_findings,
   448→        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,
   449→        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,
   450→    )
   451→
   452→
   453→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   454→# Plan mutation
   455→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   456→
   457→def apply_synthesis_to_plan(
   458→    plan: PlanModel,
   459→    state: StateModel,
   460→    synthesis: SynthesisResult,
   461→    *,
   462→    trigger: str = "manual",
   463→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   464→    """Apply parsed synthesis result to the living plan.
   465→
   466→    1. Creates/updates synthesis-clusters in plan["clusters"]
   467→    2. Marks dismissed findings as synthesized_out skips
   468→    3. Reorders queue_order to group epic members by dependency_order
   469→    4. Updates epic_synthesis_meta with snapshot hash
   470→    """
   471→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   472→    now = utc_now()
   473→    result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   474→    result.strategy_summary = synthesis.strategy_summary
   475→
   476→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   477→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
   478→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   479→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   480→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   481→    result.synthesis_version = version
   482→
   483→    # --- Update/create synthesis-clusters ------------------------------------
   484→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   485→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   486→        epic_name = f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}" if not raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else raw_name
   487→
   488→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   489→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   490→            # Update existing synthesis-cluster
   491→            existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   492→            existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
   493→            existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
   494→            existing["finding_ids"] = epic_data["finding_ids"]
   495→            existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
   496→            existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
   497→            existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
   498→            existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
   499→            existing["updated_at"] = now
   500→            existing["synthesis_version"] = version
   501→            existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   502→            # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
   503→            if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
   504→                existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
   505→            result.epics_updated += 1
   506→        else:
   507→            # Create new synthesis-cluster
   508→            cluster: Cluster = {
   509→                "name": epic_name,
   510→                "description": epic_data["thesis"],
   511→                "finding_ids": epic_data["finding_ids"],
   512→                "auto": True,
   513→                "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
   514→                "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   515→                "user_modified": False,
   516→                "created_at": now,
   517→                "updated_at": now,
   518→                # Epic fields
   519→                "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
   520→                "direction": epic_data["direction"],
   521→                "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
   522→                "supersedes": [],
   523→                "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
   524→                "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   525→                "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   526→                "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   527→                "source_clusters": [],
   528→                "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   529→                "synthesis_version": version,
   530→            }
   531→            clusters[epic_name] = cluster
   532→            result.epics_created += 1
   533→
   534→    # --- Dismiss findings ---------------------------------------------------
   535→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
   536→    for df in synthesis.dismissed_findings:
   537→        fid = df.finding_id
   538→        dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   539→        # Remove from queue, add to skipped as synthesized_out
   540→        if fid in order:
   541→            order.remove(fid)
   542→        skipped[fid] = {
   543→            "finding_id": fid,
   544→            "kind": "synthesized_out",
   545→            "reason": df.reason,
   546→            "note": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   547→            "attestation": None,
   548→            "created_at": now,
   549→            "review_after": None,
   550→            "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   551→        }
   552→        result.findings_dismissed += 1
   553→
   554→    # Also handle per-epic dismissed lists
   555→    for epic_data in synthesis.epics:
   556→        for fid in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
   557→            if fid not in dismissed_ids and fid in order:
   558→                order.remove(fid)
   559→                dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   560→                skipped[fid] = {
   561→                    "finding_id": fid,
   562→                    "kind": "synthesized_out",
   563→                    "reason": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   564→                    "note": None,
   565→                    "attestation": None,
   566→                    "created_at": now,
   567→                    "review_after": None,
   568→                    "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   569→                }
   570→                result.findings_dismissed += 1
   571→
   572→    # --- Reorder queue: epic findings grouped by dependency_order -----------
   573→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   574→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   575→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   576→        for fid in epic_data["finding_ids"]:
   577→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids and fid not in dismissed_ids:
   578→                epic_finding_ids.add(fid)
   579→                epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   580→
   581→    # Rebuild order: epic items first (by dependency), then non-epic items in original order
   582→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_finding_ids]
   583→    # Insert epic items at the front (after synthesis::pending if present)
   584→    new_order: list[str] = []
   585→    # Keep synthesis::pending at front if present (it will be pruned next sync)
   586→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in non_epic_items:
   587→        non_epic_items.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   588→    new_order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   589→    new_order.extend(non_epic_items)
   590→    order.clear()
   591→    order.extend(new_order)
   592→
   593→    # --- Update synthesis meta -----------------------------------------------
   594→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   595→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   596→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   597→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   598→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   599→    )
   600→
   601→    plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {
   602→        "synthesized_ids": open_review_ids,
   603→        "last_run": now,
   604→        "version": version,
   605→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   606→        "finding_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   607→        "strategy_summary": synthesis.strategy_summary,
   608→        "trigger": trigger,
   609→    }
   610→    plan["updated"] = now
   611→
   612→    return result
   613→
   614→
   615→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   616→# Top-level orchestrator
   617→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   618→
   619→@dataclass
   620→class SynthesisDeps:
   621→    """Injectable dependencies for the synthesis engine."""
   622→
   623→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
   624→
   625→
   626→def synthesize_epics(
   627→    plan: PlanModel,
   628→    state: StateModel,
   629→    *,
   630→    deps: SynthesisDeps | None = None,
   631→    dry_run: bool = False,
   632→    trigger: str = "manual",
   633→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   634→    """Run epic synthesis: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   635→
   636→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   637→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   638→
   639→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   640→    """
   641→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   642→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   643→
   644→    prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
   645→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   646→
   647→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   648→        result = SynthesisMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   649→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_findings)} findings"
   650→        return result
   651→
   652→    # Call LLM
   653→    try:
   654→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   655→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   656→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   657→        logger.error("Epic synthesis LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   658→        result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   659→        result.strategy_summary = f"Synthesis failed: {exc}"
   660→        return result
   661→
   662→    synthesis = parse_synthesis_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   663→    return apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, trigger=trigger)
   664→
   665→
   666→__all__ = [
   667→    "DismissedFinding",
   668→    "FINDING_ID_RE",
   669→    "SynthesisDeps",
   670→    "SynthesisInput",
   671→    "SynthesisMutationResult",
   672→    "SynthesisResult",
   673→    "apply_synthesis_to_plan",
   674→    "build_synthesis_prompt",
   675→    "collect_synthesis_input",
   676→    "detect_recurring_patterns",
   677→    "extract_finding_citations",
   678→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   679→    "parse_synthesis_result",
   680→    "synthesize_epics",
   681→]
   682→
