1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .batch import _do_run_batches
    13→from .external import do_external_start, do_external_submit
    14→from .import_cmd import do_import, do_validate_import
    15→from .merge import do_merge
    16→from .preflight import review_rerun_preflight
    17→from .prepare import do_prepare
    18→
    19→
    20→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    21→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    22→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    23→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    24→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    25→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    26→            continue
    27→        try:
    28→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    29→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    30→            _ = exc
    31→
    32→
    33→def _require_lang_or_exit(lang) -> None:
    34→    if lang:
    35→        return
    36→    print(
    37→        colorize("  Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", "red"),
    38→        file=sys.stderr,
    39→    )
    40→    sys.exit(1)
    41→
    42→
    43→def _mode_flags(args: argparse.Namespace) -> tuple[list[bool], object, object]:
    44→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
    45→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
    46→    external_start = bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False))
    47→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    48→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
    49→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
    50→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
    51→    flags = [
    52→        merge,
    53→        run_batches,
    54→        external_start,
    55→        external_submit,
    56→        import_mode,
    57→        bool(validate_import_file),
    58→    ]
    59→    return flags, import_file, validate_import_file
    60→
    61→
    62→def _validate_mode_selection_or_exit(
    63→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    64→    *,
    65→    mode_flags: list[bool],
    66→    import_file: object,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
    69→        print(
    70→            colorize(
    71→                "  Error: choose one review mode per command "
    72→                "(--merge | --run-batches | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
    73→                "red",
    74→            ),
    75→            file=sys.stderr,
    76→        )
    77→        sys.exit(1)
    78→
    79→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    80→    if external_submit and not import_file:
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
    84→                "red",
    85→            ),
    86→            file=sys.stderr,
    87→        )
    88→        sys.exit(2)
    89→    if external_submit and not getattr(args, "session_id", None):
    90→        print(
    91→            colorize(
    92→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
    93→                "red",
    94→            ),
    95→            file=sys.stderr,
    96→        )
    97→        sys.exit(2)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _run_review_mode(
   101→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   102→    *,
   103→    runtime,
   104→    state,
   105→    lang,
   106→    state_file,
   107→    import_file,
   108→    validate_import_file,
   109→) -> None:
   110→    if bool(getattr(args, "merge", False)):
   111→        do_merge(args)
   112→        return
   113→    if bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False)):
   114→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   115→        _do_run_batches(
   116→            args,
   117→            state,
   118→            lang,
   119→            state_file,
   120→            config=runtime.config,
   121→        )
   122→        return
   123→    if bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False)):
   124→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   125→        do_external_start(
   126→            args,
   127→            state,
   128→            lang,
   129→            config=runtime.config,
   130→        )
   131→        return
   132→    if bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False)):
   133→        review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   134→        do_external_submit(
   135→            import_file=str(import_file),
   136→            session_id=str(getattr(args, "session_id")),
   137→            state=state,
   138→            lang=lang,
   139→            state_file=state_file,
   140→            config=runtime.config,
   141→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   142→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
   143→            scan_path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
   144→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
   145→        )
   146→        return
   147→    if validate_import_file:
   148→        do_validate_import(
   149→            validate_import_file,
   150→            lang,
   151→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   152→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   153→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   154→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   155→        )
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    review_rerun_preflight(state, args, state_file=state_file)
   159→    if import_file:
   160→        do_import(
   161→            import_file,
   162→            state,
   163→            lang,
   164→            state_file,
   165→            config=runtime.config,
   166→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   167→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   168→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   169→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   170→        )
   171→        return
   172→    do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   173→
   174→
   175→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   176→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
   177→    _enable_live_review_output()
   178→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   179→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   180→    state = runtime.state
   181→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   182→    _require_lang_or_exit(lang)
   183→
   184→    mode_flags, import_file, validate_import_file = _mode_flags(args)
   185→    _validate_mode_selection_or_exit(
   186→        args,
   187→        mode_flags=mode_flags,
   188→        import_file=import_file,
   189→    )
   190→    _run_review_mode(
   191→        args,

   195→        state_file=state_file,
   196→        import_file=import_file,

   198→    )

1→"""Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import print_execution_or_reveal
    11→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    15→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import list_open_review_findings
    16→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    17→from desloppify.state import save_state, utc_now
    18→
    19→
    20→def _word_set(text: str) -> set[str]:
    21→    words = "".join(ch.lower() if ch.isalnum() else " " for ch in text).split()
    22→    return {word for word in words if len(word) >= 3}
    23→
    24→
    25→def _summary_similarity(a: str, b: str) -> float:
    26→    left = _word_set(a)
    27→    right = _word_set(b)
    28→    if not left or not right:
    29→        return 0.0
    30→    overlap = len(left & right)
    31→    union = len(left | right)
    32→    return float(overlap) / float(union) if union else 0.0
    33→
    34→
    35→def _parse_holistic_identifier(finding_id: str) -> str:
    36→    parts = [part for part in str(finding_id).split("::") if part]
    37→    if len(parts) < 2:
    38→        return ""
    39→    candidate = parts[-2].strip()
    40→    if not candidate or candidate in {"holistic", "changed", "stale", "unreviewed"}:
    41→        return ""
    42→    return candidate
    43→
    44→
    45→def _related_files_set(finding: dict) -> set[str]:
    46→    related = finding.get("detail", {}).get("related_files", [])
    47→    if not isinstance(related, list):
    48→        return set()
    49→    return {str(path).strip() for path in related if str(path).strip()}
    50→
    51→
    52→def _same_issue_concept(
    53→    left: dict,
    54→    right: dict,
    55→    *,
    56→    similarity_threshold: float,
    57→) -> bool:
    58→    left_detail = left.get("detail", {})
    59→    right_detail = right.get("detail", {})
    60→    if left_detail.get("dimension") != right_detail.get("dimension"):
    61→        return False
    62→
    63→    left_identifier = _parse_holistic_identifier(left.get("id", ""))
    64→    right_identifier = _parse_holistic_identifier(right.get("id", ""))
    65→    if left_identifier and right_identifier and left_identifier == right_identifier:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    left_summary = str(left.get("summary", "")).strip()
    69→    right_summary = str(right.get("summary", "")).strip()
    70→    similarity = _summary_similarity(left_summary, right_summary)
    71→    if similarity < similarity_threshold:
    72→        return False
    73→
    74→    left_files = _related_files_set(left)
    75→    right_files = _related_files_set(right)
    76→    if left_files and right_files and not (left_files & right_files):
    77→        return False
    78→    return True
    79→
    80→
    81→def _merge_finding_details(primary: dict, duplicate: dict) -> None:
    82→    primary_detail = primary.setdefault("detail", {})
    83→    duplicate_detail = duplicate.get("detail", {})
    84→
    85→    for field in ("related_files", "evidence"):
    86→        merged: list[str] = []
    87→        seen: set[str] = set()
    88→        for source in (primary_detail.get(field), duplicate_detail.get(field)):
    89→            if not isinstance(source, list):
    90→                continue
    91→            for item in source:
    92→                value = str(item).strip()
    93→                if not value or value in seen:
    94→                    continue
    95→                seen.add(value)
    96→                merged.append(value)
    97→        if merged:
    98→            primary_detail[field] = merged
    99→
   100→    primary_suggestion = str(primary_detail.get("suggestion", "")).strip()
   101→    duplicate_suggestion = str(duplicate_detail.get("suggestion", "")).strip()
   102→    if len(duplicate_suggestion) > len(primary_suggestion):
   103→        primary_detail["suggestion"] = duplicate_suggestion
   104→
   105→    merged_from = primary_detail.get("merged_from")
   106→    if not isinstance(merged_from, list):
   107→        merged_from = []
   108→    duplicate_id = duplicate.get("id", "")
   109→    if duplicate_id and duplicate_id not in merged_from:
   110→        merged_from.append(duplicate_id)
   111→    if merged_from:
   112→        primary_detail["merged_from"] = merged_from
   113→
   114→
   115→def do_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   116→    """Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings."""
   117→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   118→    state = runtime.state
   119→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   120→    narrative = compute_narrative(state, context=NarrativeContext(command="review"))
   121→    items = list_open_review_findings(state)
   122→
   123→    if not items:
   124→        print(colorize("\n  No review findings open.\n", "dim"))
   125→        return
   126→
   127→    try:
   128→        similarity = float(getattr(args, "similarity", 0.8))
   129→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   130→        similarity = 0.8
   131→    similarity = max(0.0, min(1.0, similarity))
   132→
   133→    open_holistic = [
   134→        finding
   135→        for finding in items
   136→        if finding.get("detector") == "review"
   137→        and finding.get("detail", {}).get("holistic")
   138→    ]
   139→    if len(open_holistic) < 2:
   140→        print(colorize("\n  Not enough holistic review findings to merge.\n", "dim"))
   141→        return
   142→
   143→    consumed: set[str] = set()
   144→    merge_groups: list[list[dict]] = []
   145→    for candidate in open_holistic:
   146→        candidate_id = candidate.get("id", "")
   147→        if not candidate_id or candidate_id in consumed:
   148→            continue
   149→        group = [candidate]
   150→        consumed.add(candidate_id)
   151→        for other in open_holistic:
   152→            other_id = other.get("id", "")
   153→            if not other_id or other_id in consumed:
   154→                continue
   155→            if _same_issue_concept(
   156→                candidate,
   157→                other,
   158→                similarity_threshold=similarity,
   159→            ):
   160→                consumed.add(other_id)
   161→                group.append(other)
   162→        if len(group) > 1:
   163→            merge_groups.append(group)
   164→
   165→    if not merge_groups:
   166→        print(
   167→            colorize(
   168→                "\n  No duplicate issue concepts found at the current similarity threshold.\n",
   169→                "dim",
   170→            )
   171→        )
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   175→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   176→    timestamp = utc_now()
   177→    merged_pairs: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
   178→    for group in merge_groups:
   179→        ranked = sorted(
   180→            group,
   181→            key=lambda finding: (finding_weight(finding)[0], finding.get("id", "")),
   182→            reverse=True,
   183→        )
   184→        primary = ranked[0]
   185→        duplicates = ranked[1:]
   186→        merged_pairs.append((primary.get("id", ""), [d.get("id", "") for d in duplicates]))
   187→        if dry_run:
   188→            continue
   189→        for duplicate in duplicates:
   190→            _merge_finding_details(primary, duplicate)
   191→            duplicate["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   192→            duplicate["resolved_at"] = timestamp
   193→            duplicate["note"] = f"merged into {primary.get('id', '')}"
   194→            duplicate["resolution_attestation"] = {
   195→                "kind": "issue_merge",
   196→                "text": "Merged conceptually duplicate review finding",
   197→                "attested_at": timestamp,
   198→                "scan_verified": False,
   199→            }
   200→        primary_detail = primary.setdefault("detail", {})
   201→        primary_detail["merged_at"] = timestamp
   202→
   203→    print(
   204→        colorize(
   205→            f"\n  Merge groups: {len(merge_groups)} | "
   206→            f"duplicate findings: {sum(len(group) - 1 for group in merge_groups)}",
   207→            "bold",
   208→        )
   209→    )
   210→    for index, (primary_id, duplicate_ids) in enumerate(merged_pairs, 1):
   211→        preview = ", ".join(duplicate_ids[:3])
   212→        if len(duplicate_ids) > 3:
   213→            preview = f"{preview}, +{len(duplicate_ids) - 3} more"
   214→        print(colorize(f"  [{index}] keep {primary_id}", "dim"))
   215→        print(colorize(f"      merge {preview}", "dim"))
   216→
   217→    if dry_run:
   218→        print(colorize("\n  Dry run only: no state changes written.\n", "yellow"))
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    save_state(state, state_file)
   222→    print(colorize("\n  State updated with merged issue groups.", "green"))
   223→    try:
   224→        _merge_plan = load_plan()
   225→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   226→        _merge_plan = None
   227→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, _merge_plan)
   228→    write_query(
   229→        {
   230→            "command": "review",
   231→            "action": "merge",
   232→            "groups": len(merge_groups),
   233→            "duplicates_merged": sum(len(group) - 1 for group in merge_groups),
   234→            "next_command": "desloppify show review --status open",
   235→            "narrative": narrative,
   236→        }
   237→    )
   238→

    76→    """Return review packet config with score-target hints removed."""

    83→    """Resolve per-batch file cap from config (0/negative => unlimited)."""
    84→    raw = (config or {}).get("review_batch_max_files", DEFAULT_REVIEW_BATCH_MAX_FILES)
    85→    try:

   129→def _default_blind_packet_path() -> Path:
   130→    return get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / "review_packet_blind.json"
   131→
   132→
   133→def _is_sha256_hex(raw: object) -> bool:
   134→    return (
   135→        isinstance(raw, str)
   136→        and len(raw) == 64
   137→        and all(ch in "0123456789abcdefABCDEF" for ch in raw)
   138→    )
   139→
   140→
   141→def _hash_file_sha256(path: Path) -> str | None:
   142→    try:
   143→        data = path.read_bytes()
   144→    except OSError:
   145→        return None
   146→    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   147→
   148→
   149→def _resolve_packet_path(raw_path: object) -> Path | None:
   150→    if not isinstance(raw_path, str):
   151→        return None
   152→    text = raw_path.strip()
   153→    if not text:
   154→        return None
   155→    path = Path(text)
   156→    return path if path.is_absolute() else get_project_root() / path
   157→
   158→
   159→def _assessment_provenance_status(
   160→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   161→    *,
   162→    import_file: str,
   163→) -> AssessmentProvenanceModel:
   164→    """Evaluate whether assessments come from a trusted blind batch artifact."""
   165→    provenance = findings_data["provenance"]
   166→    if not provenance:
   167→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   168→            trusted=False,
   169→            reason="missing provenance metadata",
   170→            import_file=import_file,
   171→        )
   172→
   173→    kind = str(provenance.get("kind", "")).strip()
   174→    if kind != _BLIND_PROVENANCE_KIND:
   175→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   176→            trusted=False,
   177→            reason=f"unsupported provenance kind: {kind or '<missing>'}",
   178→            import_file=import_file,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    if provenance.get("blind") is not True:
   182→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   183→            trusted=False,
   184→            reason="provenance is not marked blind=true",
   185→            import_file=import_file,
   186→        )
   187→
   188→    runner = str(provenance.get("runner", "")).strip().lower()
   189→    if runner not in _SUPPORTED_BLIND_REVIEW_RUNNERS:
   190→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   191→            trusted=False,
   192→            reason=f"unsupported runner in provenance: {runner or '<missing>'}",
   193→            import_file=import_file,
   194→        )
   195→
   196→    packet_hash = provenance.get("packet_sha256")
   197→    if not _is_sha256_hex(packet_hash):
   198→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   199→            trusted=False,
   200→            reason="missing or invalid packet_sha256 in provenance",
   201→            import_file=import_file,
   202→        )
   203→
   204→    packet_path = _resolve_packet_path(provenance.get("packet_path"))
   205→    if packet_path is None:
   206→        packet_path = _default_blind_packet_path()
   207→    if not packet_path.exists():
   208→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   209→            trusted=False,
   210→            reason=f"blind packet not found: {packet_path}",
   211→            import_file=import_file,
   212→        )
   213→    observed_hash = _hash_file_sha256(packet_path)
   214→    if observed_hash is None:
   215→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   216→            trusted=False,
   217→            reason=f"unable to hash blind packet: {packet_path}",
   218→            import_file=import_file,
   219→        )
   220→    if observed_hash != packet_hash:
   221→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   222→            trusted=False,
   223→            reason=(
   224→                "blind packet hash mismatch "
   225→                f"(expected {packet_hash[:12]}..., got {observed_hash[:12]}...)"
   226→            ),
   227→            import_file=import_file,
   228→        )
   229→
   230→    return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   231→        trusted=True,
   232→        reason="trusted blind subagent provenance",
   233→        runner=runner,
   234→        packet_path=str(packet_path),
   235→        packet_sha256=packet_hash,
   236→        import_file=import_file,
   237→    )

   693→) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   694→    """Load and normalize review import payload to object format.
   695→
   696→    Raises ``ImportPayloadLoadError`` when validation fails.
   697→    """
   698→    data, errors = _parse_and_validate_import(
   699→        import_file,
   700→        lang_name=lang_name,
   701→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   702→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   703→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   704→        attested_external=attested_external,
   705→        manual_override=manual_override,
   706→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   707→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   708→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   709→    )
   710→    if errors:
   711→        raise ImportPayloadLoadError(errors)
   712→    assert data is not None  # guaranteed when errors is empty
   713→    return data
   714→
   715→
   716→def print_import_load_errors(
   717→    errors: list[str],
   718→    *,
   719→    import_file: str,
   720→    colorize_fn,
   721→) -> None:
   722→    """Print import payload validation errors and actionable hints."""
   723→    for err in errors:
   724→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {err}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   725→    _print_import_error_hints(errors, import_file=import_file, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   726→
   727→
   728→def assessment_policy_from_payload(payload: ReviewImportPayload) -> AssessmentImportPolicy:
   729→    """Return parsed assessment policy metadata from a loaded import payload."""
   730→    policy = payload[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY]
   731→    if isinstance(policy, dict):
   732→        return policy
   733→    return AssessmentImportPolicyModel().to_dict()
   734→
   735→
   736→def assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   737→    payload: ReviewImportPayload,
   738→) -> AssessmentImportPolicyModel:
   739→    """Return typed assessment policy metadata from a loaded import payload."""
   740→    return AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(assessment_policy_from_payload(payload))
   741→
   742→
   743→def assessment_mode_label(policy: AssessmentImportPolicy) -> str:
   744→    """Return a user-facing label for the selected assessment import mode."""
   745→    mode = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy).mode.strip().lower()
   746→    return _ASSESSMENT_MODE_LABELS.get(mode, f"unknown ({mode or 'none'})")
   747→

   757→    assessments_present = bool(policy_model.assessments_present)
   758→    if not assessments_present and mode == "none":
   759→        return

560→def _parse_and_validate_import(
   561→    import_file: str,
   562→    *,
   563→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   564→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   565→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   566→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   567→    attested_external: bool = False,
   568→    manual_override: bool = False,
   569→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   570→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   571→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   572→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   573→    """Parse and validate a review import file (pure function).
   574→
   575→    Returns ``(data, errors)`` where *data* is the normalized payload on
   576→    success, or ``None`` when errors prevent import.
   577→    """
   578→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   579→    if not findings_path.exists():
   580→        return None, [f"file not found: {import_file}"]
   581→    try:
   582→        findings_data = json.loads(findings_path.read_text())
   583→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   584→        return None, [f"error reading findings: {exc}"]
   585→
   586→    if isinstance(findings_data, list):
   587→        findings_data = {"findings": findings_data}
   588→
   589→    if not isinstance(findings_data, dict):
   590→        return None, ["findings file must contain a JSON array or object"]
   591→
   592→    if "findings" not in findings_data:
   593→        return None, ["findings object must contain a 'findings' key"]
   594→    normalized_findings_data, shape_errors = _normalize_import_payload_shape(
   595→        findings_data
   596→    )
   597→    if shape_errors:
   598→        return None, shape_errors
   599→    assert normalized_findings_data is not None
   600→
   601→    override_enabled, override_attest = resolve_override_context(
   602→        manual_override=manual_override,
   603→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   604→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   605→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   606→    )
   607→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
   608→        return None, [
   609→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
   610→        ]
   611→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
   612→        return None, [
   613→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   614→            "attested score imports require fully valid findings payloads"
   615→        ]
   616→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
   617→        return None, [
   618→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   619→            "manual score imports require fully valid findings payloads"
   620→        ]
   621→    findings_data, policy_errors = _apply_assessment_import_policy(
   622→        normalized_findings_data,
   623→        import_file=import_file,
   624→        attested_external=attested_external,
   625→        attested_attest=override_attest,
   626→        manual_override=override_enabled,
   627→        manual_attest=override_attest,
   628→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   629→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   630→    )
   631→    if policy_errors:
   632→        return None, policy_errors
   633→    assert findings_data is not None
   634→
   635→    missing_feedback, missing_low_score_findings = _validate_assessment_feedback(
   636→        findings_data
   637→    )
   638→    if missing_low_score_findings:
   639→        if override_enabled:
   640→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   641→                return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   642→            return findings_data, []
   643→        return None, [
   644→            f"assessments below {LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include at "
   645→            "least one finding for that same dimension with a concrete suggestion. "
   646→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_low_score_findings)}"
   647→        ]
   648→
   649→    if missing_feedback:
   650→        if override_enabled:
   651→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   652→                return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   653→            return findings_data, []
   654→        return None, [
   655→            f"assessments below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include explicit feedback "
   656→            "(finding with same dimension and non-empty suggestion, or "
   657→            "dimension_notes evidence for that dimension). "
   658→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_feedback)}"
   659→        ]
   660→
   661→    schema_errors = _validate_holistic_findings_schema(
   662→        findings_data,
   663→        lang_name=lang_name,
   664→    )
   665→    if schema_errors and not allow_partial:
   666→        visible_errors = schema_errors[:10]
   667→        remaining = len(schema_errors) - len(visible_errors)
   668→        errors = [
   669→            "findings schema validation failed for holistic import. "
   670→            "Fix payload or rerun with --allow-partial to continue."
   671→        ]
   672→        errors.extend(visible_errors)
   673→        if remaining > 0:
   674→            errors.append(f"... {remaining} additional schema error(s) omitted")
   675→        return None, errors
   676→
   677→    return findings_data, []
   678→
   679→

337→def _apply_assessment_import_policy(
   338→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   339→    *,
   340→    import_file: str,
   341→    attested_external: bool,
   342→    attested_attest: str | None,
   343→    manual_override: bool,
   344→    manual_attest: str | None,
   345→    trusted_assessment_source: bool,
   346→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None,
   347→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   348→    """Apply trust gating for assessment imports (findings import always allowed)."""
   349→    assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
   350→    has_assessments = bool(assessments)
   351→    assessment_count = len(assessments) if has_assessments else 0
   352→    provenance_status = _assessment_provenance_status(
   353→        findings_data, import_file=import_file
   354→    )
   355→    policy = AssessmentImportPolicyModel(
   356→        assessments_present=has_assessments,
   357→        assessment_count=int(assessment_count),
   358→        trusted=False,
   359→        mode="none",
   360→        reason="",
   361→        provenance=provenance_status,
   362→    )
   363→
   364→    def _attach_policy(payload: ReviewImportPayload) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   365→        normalized = dict(payload)
   366→        normalized[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY] = policy.to_dict()
   367→        return normalized
   368→
   369→    if not has_assessments:
   370→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   371→
   372→    if trusted_assessment_source:
   373→        policy = replace(
   374→            policy,
   375→            mode="trusted_internal",
   376→            trusted=True,
   377→            reason=(trusted_assessment_label or "trusted internal run-batches import"),
   378→        )
   379→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   380→
   381→    if attested_external:
   382→        normalized_attest = _validate_attested_external_attestation(attested_attest)
   383→        if normalized_attest is None:
   384→            return None, [
   385→                "--attested-external requires --attest containing both "
   386→                "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'"
   387→            ]
   388→        if provenance_status.trusted is not True:
   389→            return None, [
   390→                "--attested-external requires valid blind packet provenance "
   391→                f"(current status: {provenance_status.reason or 'untrusted provenance'})"
   392→            ]
   393→        runner = provenance_status.runner.strip().lower()
   394→        if runner not in _ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_RUNNERS:
   395→            return None, [
   396→                "--attested-external currently supports runner='claude' provenance only"
   397→            ]
   398→        policy = replace(
   399→            policy,
   400→            mode="attested_external",
   401→            trusted=True,
   402→            reason="attested external blind subagent provenance",
   403→            attest=normalized_attest,
   404→        )
   405→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   406→
   407→    if manual_override:
   408→        if not isinstance(manual_attest, str) or not manual_attest.strip():
   409→            return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   410→        policy = replace(
   411→            policy,
   412→            mode="manual_override",
   413→            reason="manual override attested by operator",
   414→            attest=manual_attest.strip(),
   415→        )
   416→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   417→
   418→    policy = replace(policy, mode="findings_only")
   419→    if findings_data["provenance"]:
   420→        provenance_reason = provenance_status.reason.strip()
   421→        if provenance_status.trusted is True:
   422→            policy = replace(
   423→                policy,
   424→                reason=(
   425→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust even when provenance appears valid; "
   426→                    "run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   427→                ),
   428→            )
   429→        elif provenance_reason:
   430→            policy = replace(
   431→                policy,
   432→                reason=(
   433→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust "
   434→                    f"({provenance_reason}); run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   435→                ),
   436→            )
   437→        else:
   438→            policy = replace(
   439→                policy,
   440→                reason=(
   441→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust; "
   442→                    "run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   443→                ),
   444→            )
   445→    else:
   446→        policy = replace(
   447→            policy,
   448→            reason="missing trusted run-batches source; imported findings only",
   449→        )
   450→    payload = dict(findings_data)
   451→    payload["assessments"] = {}
   452→    payload[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY] = policy.to_dict()
   453→    return payload, []
   454→
   455→
   456→def _has_non_empty_strings(items: object) -> bool:
   457→    """Return True when ``items`` is a list with at least one non-empty string."""
   458→    return isinstance(items, list) and any(
   459→        isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in items
   460→    )
   461→
   462→
   463→def _validate_holistic_findings_schema(
   464→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   465→    *,
   466→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   467→) -> list[str]:
   468→    """Validate strict holistic finding schema expected by issue import."""
   469→    findings = findings_data["findings"]
   470→
   471→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   472→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip():
   473→        _, dimension_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   474→        allowed_dimensions = set(dimension_prompts)
   475→
   476→    errors: list[str] = []
   477→    for idx, entry in enumerate(findings):
   478→        _normalized: ReviewFindingPayload | None
   479→        _normalized, entry_errors = validate_review_finding_payload(
   480→            entry,
   481→            label=f"findings[{idx}]",
   482→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions or None,
   483→            allow_dismissed=True,
   484→        )
   485→        for message in entry_errors:
   486→            if (
   487→                "is not allowed" in message
   488→                and lang_name
   489→                and "dimension '" in message
   490→            ):
   491→                message = message.replace(
   492→                    "is not allowed",
   493→                    f"is not valid for language '{lang_name}'",
   494→                )
   495→            errors.append(message)
   496→    return errors
   497→
   498→
   499→def _feedback_dimensions_from_findings(findings: object) -> set[str]:
   500→    """Return dimensions with explicit improvement guidance in findings payload."""
   501→    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   502→        return set()
   503→    dims: set[str] = set()
   504→    for entry in findings:
   505→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   506→            continue
   507→        dim = entry.get("dimension")
   508→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   509→            continue
   510→        suggestion = entry.get("suggestion")
   511→        if isinstance(suggestion, str) and suggestion.strip():
   512→            dims.add(dim.strip())
   513→    return dims
   514→
   515→
   516→def _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(dimension_notes: object) -> set[str]:
   517→    """Return dimensions with concrete review evidence in dimension_notes payload."""
   518→    if not isinstance(dimension_notes, dict):
   519→        return set()
   520→    dims: set[str] = set()
   521→    for dim, note in dimension_notes.items():
   522→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   523→            continue
   524→        if not isinstance(note, dict):
   525→            continue
   526→        if not _has_non_empty_strings(note.get("evidence")):
   527→            continue
   528→        dims.add(dim.strip())
   529→    return dims
   530→
   531→
   532→def _validate_assessment_feedback(
   533→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   534→) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   535→    """Return dimensions missing required feedback and required low-score findings."""
   536→    assessments = findings_data["assessments"]

   782→        for idx in successful_indexes
   783→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   784→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   785→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   786→    )
   787→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   788→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   789→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   790→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   791→    }
   792→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   793→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   794→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   795→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   796→    if reviewed_files:
   797→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   798→        print(
   799→            colorize_fn(
   800→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   801→                "dim",
   802→            )
   803→        )
   804→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   805→        runner=runner,
   806→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   807→        run_stamp=stamp,
   808→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   809→    )
   810→    merged_assessment_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   811→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   812→    )
   813→    merged_finding_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   814→        [
   815→            finding.get("dimension")
   816→            for finding in (merged.get("findings") or [])
   817→            if isinstance(finding, dict)
   818→        ]
   819→    )
   820→    merged_imported_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   821→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_finding_dims
   822→    )
   823→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   824→    missing_after_import = _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   825→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   826→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   827→        scan_path=scan_path,
   828→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   829→    )
   830→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   831→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   832→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   833→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   834→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   835→    }
   836→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_findings_merged.json"
   837→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   838→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   839→    _print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   840→
   841→    try:
   842→        do_import_fn(
   843→            str(merged_path),
   844→            state,
   845→            lang,
   846→            state_file,
   847→            config=config,
   848→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   849→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
```

744→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   745→    *,
   746→    runner: str,
   747→    blind_packet_path: Path,
   748→    run_stamp: str,
   749→    batch_indexes: list[int],
   750→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   751→    """Build provenance payload used to trust assessment-bearing imports."""
   752→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_packet_path)
   753→    batch_indexes_1 = sorted({int(index) + 1 for index in batch_indexes})
   754→    return {
   755→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   756→        "blind": True,
   757→        "runner": runner,
   758→        "run_stamp": run_stamp,
   759→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   760→        "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
   761→        "batch_indexes": batch_indexes_1,
   762→        "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
   763→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   764→    }
   765→
   766→
   767→def selected_batch_indexes(
   768→    *,
   769→    raw_selection: str | None,
   770→    batch_count: int,
   771→    parse_fn,
   772→    colorize_fn,
   773→) -> list[int]:
   774→    """Validate selected batch indexes or exit with a CLI error."""
   775→    try:
   776→        selected = parse_fn(raw_selection, batch_count)
   777→    except ValueError as exc:
   778→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   779→        sys.exit(2)
   780→    if selected:
   781→        return selected
   782→    print(colorize_fn("  Error: no batches selected", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   783→    sys.exit(2)
   784→
   785→
   786→def prepare_run_artifacts(
   787→    *,
   788→    stamp: str,
   789→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   790→    batches: list[dict],
   791→    packet_path: Path,
   792→    run_root: Path,
   793→    repo_root: Path,
   794→    build_prompt_fn,
   795→    safe_write_text_fn,
   796→    colorize_fn,
   797→) -> tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]:
   798→    """Build prompt/output/log paths and persist prompts for selected batches."""
   799→    run_dir = run_root / stamp
   800→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   801→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   802→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   803→    prompts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
```

class AssessmentProvenanceModel:
    """Typed provenance status model for assessment import trust checks."""

    trusted: bool = False
    reason: str = ""
    import_file: str = ""
    runner: str = ""
    packet_path: str = ""
    packet_sha256: str = ""

    @classmethod
    def from_mapping(
        cls, payload: AssessmentProvenanceStatus | dict[str, Any] | None
    ) -> AssessmentProvenanceModel:
        data = payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
        return cls(
            trusted=bool(data.get("trusted", False)),
            reason=str(data.get("reason", "") or ""),
            import_file=str(data.get("import_file", "") or ""),
            runner=str(data.get("runner", "") or ""),
            packet_path=str(data.get("packet_path", "") or ""),
--
class AssessmentImportPolicyModel:
    """Typed assessment import policy model used by review import flows."""

    assessments_present: bool = False
    assessment_count: int = 0
    trusted: bool = False
    mode: str = "none"
    reason: str = ""
    provenance: AssessmentProvenanceModel = field(
        default_factory=AssessmentProvenanceModel
    )
    attest: str | None = None

    @classmethod
    def from_mapping(
        cls, payload: AssessmentImportPolicy | dict[str, Any] | None
    ) -> AssessmentImportPolicyModel:
        data = payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
        attest = data.get("attest")
        return cls(
            assessments_present=bool(data.get("assessments_present", False)),

81→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(

    84→    assessment_keys: set[str],
    85→) -> int:
    86→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""

    88→        return 0

    90→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
    91→        return 0

   102→        payload["provisional_override"] = True

Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile a comprehensive research report.

## Investigation Summary: Review Import Provenance Trust Mechanism

I've completed a thorough investigation of the review import code path. Here's what I found:

### **The Core Issue**

The provenance/trust mechanism is **asymmetric by design**:
- `--run-batches` imports are **durable** (scores stick around)
- Manual imports are **provisional** (scores reset on next scan)

This is controlled by a single boolean flag: `trusted_assessment_source` passed to `do_import()`.

### **1. Trust Mechanism: How `--run-batches` Works (Durable Scores)**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batches.py:804-851`

When `--run-batches` completes successfully:

```python
# Line 849-850 in batches.py
do_import_fn(
    str(merged_path),
    state,
    lang,
    state_file,
    config=config,
    allow_partial=allow_partial,
    trusted_assessment_source=True,  # ← KEY DIFFERENCE
    trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
)
```

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/runner_helpers.py:744-764`

The `build_batch_import_provenance()` function creates a provenance payload containing:
```python
{
    "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
    "blind": True,
    "runner": runner,              # "codex" in your case
    "run_stamp": run_stamp,        # "20260301_181855"
    "created_at": ISO timestamp,
    "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
    "batch_indexes": [1,2,3...12],
    "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
    "packet_sha256": HASH          # ← Cryptographic anchor
}
```

This provenance is attached to the merged payload at `merged["provenance"]` (line 804).

### **2. Trust Decision Logic**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/import_helpers.py:337-453`

The function `_apply_assessment_import_policy()` determines whether scores are trusted:

```python
if trusted_assessment_source:
    policy = replace(
        policy,
        mode="trusted_internal",    # ← Sets to DURABLE mode
        trusted=True,
        reason=(trusted_assessment_label or "trusted internal run-batches import"),
    )
    return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
```

When `trusted_assessment_source=True`, the assessment import mode becomes `"trusted_internal"` which results in **durable scores** (no provisional flag set).

When `trusted_assessment_source=False` (manual import), the code checks for provenance validation (line 388-405), and if that fails or if no explicit trust flags are provided, it falls through to "findings_only" mode (line 418), which **strips all assessments from the payload** (line 451).

### **3. The Provisional Flag Mechanism**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/import_cmd.py:81-106 & 213-224`

In `do_import()`, scores are marked provisional ONLY if the import mode is `"manual_override"`:

```python
if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
    provisional_count = _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
        working_state,
        assessment_keys=imported_assessment_keys,
    )
elif assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
    _clear_provisional_override_flags(...)  # ← Clears ANY provisional flags
```

The provisional flag structure (lines 100-103):
```python
payload["provisional_override"] = True
payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan  # Current scan_count + 1
payload["source"] = "manual_override"
```

### **4. Why Manual Import Fails**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/import_helpers.py:418-453`

When you manually import a merged.json file **without** `--run-batches`:
1. `trusted_assessment_source=False` (default)
2. Code checks the provenance field (line 419-436)
3. **The provenance in your merged.json doesn't exist yet** because it hasn't been created by `build_batch_import_provenance()`
4. Falls through to "findings_only" mode (line 418)
5. **All assessments are stripped** (line 451): `payload["assessments"] = {}`
6. Only findings are imported

The error message hint (lines 424-427) is telling:
```python
"run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
```

### **5. What You Have in Your Run Directory**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/.desloppify/subagents/runs/20260301_181855/results/merged.json`

Your merged.json contains:
- `assessments` dict (20 dimensions with scores)
- `findings` array (100+ findings with detailed evidence)
- `dimension_notes` dict
- **Missing:** `provenance` field (because it wasn't added by the orchestration code before the broken pipe)

### **The Two Solution Paths**

#### **Option A: Add `--import-run <run-dir>` Command** (Recommended)

Create a new subcommand that:
1. Takes a run directory path (e.g., `.desloppify/subagents/runs/20260301_181855/`)
2. Reads all successful batch result files from `results/batch-*.raw.txt`
3. Calls `_merge_batch_results()` to merge them (same as `--run-batches` does)
4. Reads metadata from `run_summary.json` to get:
   - `run_stamp`
   - `runner`
   - `successful_batches`
5. Calls `build_batch_import_provenance()` with those values to add provenance
6. Calls `do_import()` with `trusted_assessment_source=True`

**Key files to modify:**
- Create: `desloppify/app/commands/review/import_run.py` (new module)
- Modify: `desloppify/app/commands/review/entrypoint.py` (add mode handling)
- Modify: `desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py` (add `--import-run` flag)

#### **Option B: Fix Merged.json Provenance Manually**

Manually edit the merged.json before import to add provenance:

```python
# Get these from run_summary.json
"provenance": {
    "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
    "blind": true,
    "runner": "codex",
    "run_stamp": "20260301_181855",
    "created_at": "2026-03-01T19:31:00+00:00",
    "batch_count": 12,
    "batch_indexes": [1, 2, 3, ..., 12],
    "packet_path": [REDACTED],
    "packet_sha256": "<compute SHA256 of blind packet>"
}
```

Then run:
```bash
desloppify review --import .desloppify/subagents/runs/20260301_181855/results/merged.json --attested-external --attest "..."
```

**But this would still be "attested_external" mode (requires user attestation), not fully durable.**

### **Key Code Locations Summary**

| Mechanism | File | Lines |
|-----------|------|-------|
| Trust decision logic | `import_helpers.py` | 337-453 |
| Provisional flag setting | `import_cmd.py` | 81-106, 213-224 |
| Batch provenance building | `runner_helpers.py` | 744-764 |
| Provenance validation | `import_helpers.py` | 159-237 |
| Run-batches orchestration | `batches.py` | 804-851 |
| Assessment policy enforcement | `import_cmd.py` | 288-296 (warning) |

### **Recommendation**

Implement **Option A**: Create `--import-run` that:
1. Replicates the batch merge/provenance logic from `--run-batches` orchestration
2. Works on already-completed runs (idempotent)
3. Handles broken pipe / partial completion scenarios gracefully
4. Creates truly durable imports (mode="trusted_internal")

This would be the minimal, maintainable fix that leverages existing infrastructure.

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 2 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Improve code quality by maximising the **strict score** honestly.
    19→
    20→**The main thing you do is run `desloppify next`** — it tells you exactly what to fix and how. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Keep going.
    21→
    22→Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
    23→
    24→## 2. The Workflow
    25→
    26→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    27→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    28→
    29→### Outer loop — scan and check
    30→
    31→```bash
    32→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    33→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    34→```
    35→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    36→
    37→### Inner loop — fix issues
    38→
    39→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    40→
    41→```
    42→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    43→2. Fix the issue in code
    44→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    45→```
    46→
    47→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    48→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    49→
    50→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    51→```bash
    52→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    53→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    54→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    55→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    56→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # skip / hide from next
    57→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    58→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    59→```
    60→
    61→### Subjective reviews
    62→
    63→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    64→If you need to trigger one manually:
    65→```bash
    66→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    67→```
    68→
    69→### Other useful commands
    70→
    71→```bash
    72→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    73→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    74→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    75→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    76→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    77→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    78→```
    79→
    80→## 3. Reference
    81→
    82→### How scoring works
    83→
    84→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    85→
    86→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    87→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    88→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    89→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
    90→
    91→### Subjective reviews in detail
    92→
    93→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
    94→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
    95→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
    96→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
    97→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
    98→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
    99→
   100→### Key concepts
   101→
   102→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   103→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   104→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   105→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   106→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   107→
   108→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   109→
   110→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   111→
   112→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   113→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   114→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   115→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   116→
   117→## Prerequisite
   118→
   119→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   120→
   121→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   122→

   146→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   147→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   148→    if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):

   155→        "overall": scores.overall,
   156→        "objective": scores.objective,
   157→        "verified": scores.verified,
   158→    }

   162→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(

   164→) -> bool:

   185→    try:

   199→        if seeded:

   202→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   203→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   204→            dirty = True

   206→        if dirty:

   281→    prev_strict: float | None
   282→    prev_verified: float | None
   283→    prev_dim_scores: dict[str, object]
   284→
   285→
   286→@dataclass
   287→class ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   288→    """Noise budget settings and hidden finding counts for this scan."""
   289→
   290→    noise_budget: int
   291→    global_noise_budget: int
   292→    budget_warning: str | None
   293→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]
   294→    hidden_total: int
   295→
   296→
   297→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   298→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   299→    config: dict[str, object],
   300→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   301→    lang: LangRun | None,
   302→) -> LangRun | None:
   303→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   304→    if not lang:
   305→        return None
   306→
   307→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   308→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   309→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   310→        lang,
   311→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   312→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   313→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   314→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   315→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   316→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   317→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   318→        ),
   319→    )
   320→
   321→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   322→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   323→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   324→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   325→    }
   326→    return runtime_lang
   327→
   328→
   329→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   330→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   331→    *,
   332→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   333→) -> int:
   334→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   335→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   336→
   337→    reset_keys = {
   338→        key.strip()
   339→        for key in assessments
   340→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   341→    }
   342→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   343→
   344→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   345→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   346→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   347→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   348→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   349→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   350→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   351→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   352→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   353→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   354→            payload.pop("components", None)
   355→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   356→            continue
   357→        assessments[key] = {
   358→            "score": 0.0,
   359→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   360→            "assessed_at": now,
   361→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   362→            "placeholder": True,
   363→        }
   364→    return len(reset_keys)
   365→
   366→
   367→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   368→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   369→) -> int:
   370→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   371→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   372→
   373→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   374→    expired = 0
   375→    for payload in assessments.values():
   376→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   377→            continue
   378→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   379→            continue
   380→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   381→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   382→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   383→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   384→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   385→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   386→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   387→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   388→        payload.pop("components", None)
   389→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   390→        expired += 1
   391→    return expired
   392→
   393→
   394→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   395→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   396→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   397→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   398→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   399→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   400→    path = Path(args.path)
   401→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   402→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   403→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   404→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   405→        state
   406→    )
   407→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   408→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   409→            state,
   410→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   411→        )
   412→
   413→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   414→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   415→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   416→
   417→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   418→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   419→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   420→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   421→
   422→    return ScanRuntime(
   423→        args=args,
   424→        state_path=state_file,
   425→        state=state,
   426→        path=path,
   427→        config=config,
   428→        lang=lang,
   429→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   430→        profile=profile,
   431→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   432→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   433→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   434→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   435→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   440→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   441→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   442→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   443→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   444→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   445→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   446→        return findings
   447→
   448→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   449→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   450→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   451→    )
   452→    if not stale:
   453→        return findings
   454→
   455→    augmented = list(findings)
   456→    augmented.extend(stale)
   457→    for stale_finding in stale:
   458→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   459→    return augmented
   460→
   461→
   462→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   463→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   464→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   465→    *,
   466→    decay_scans: int,
   467→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   468→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   469→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   470→        findings,
   471→        state=runtime.state,
   472→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   473→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   474→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   475→    )
   476→
   477→
   478→def run_scan_generation(
   479→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   480→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   481→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   482→    enable_file_cache()
   483→    enable_parse_cache()
   484→    try:
   485→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   486→            runtime.path,
   487→            lang=runtime.lang,
   488→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   489→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   490→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   491→                profile=runtime.profile,
   492→            ),
   493→        )
   494→    finally:
   495→        disable_parse_cache()
   496→        disable_file_cache()
   497→
   498→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   499→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   500→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   501→    )
   502→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   503→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   504→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   505→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   506→    )
   507→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   508→        findings,
   509→        runtime,
   510→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   511→    )
   512→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   513→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   514→
   515→
   516→def merge_scan_results(
   517→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   518→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   519→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   520→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   521→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   522→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   523→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   524→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   525→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   526→
   527→    if not path_changed:
   528→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   529→    else:
   530→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   531→    prev_dim_scores = (
   532→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   533→    )
   534→
   535→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   536→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   537→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   538→
   539→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   540→
   541→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   542→        runtime.state,
   543→        findings,
   544→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   545→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   546→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   547→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   548→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   549→            potentials=potentials,
   550→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   551→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   552→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   553→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   554→        ),
   555→    )
   556→
   557→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   558→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   559→    )
   560→    state_mod.save_state(
   561→        runtime.state,
   562→        runtime.state_path,
   563→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   564→    )
   565→
   566→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   567→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   568→
   569→    return ScanMergeResult(
   570→        diff=diff,
   571→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   572→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   573→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   574→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   575→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   576→    )
   577→
   578→
   579→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   580→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   581→    config: dict[str, object],
   582→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   583→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   584→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   585→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   586→    )
   587→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   588→    open_findings = [
   589→        finding
   590→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   591→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   592→        ).values()
   593→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   594→    ]
   595→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   596→        open_findings,
   597→        budget=noise_budget,
   598→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   599→    )
   600→
   601→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   602→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   603→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   604→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   605→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   606→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   607→    )
   608→
   609→
   610→def persist_reminder_history(
   611→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   612→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   613→) -> None:
   614→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   615→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   616→        return
   617→
   618→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   619→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   620→    state_mod.save_state(
   621→        runtime.state,
   622→        runtime.state_path,
   623→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   624→    )
   625→
   626→
   627→__all__ = [
   628→    "ScanStateContractError",
   629→    "ScanMergeResult",
   630→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   631→    "ScanRuntime",
   632→    "merge_scan_results",
   633→    "persist_reminder_history",
   634→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   635→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   636→    "run_scan_generation",
   637→]
   638→

   100→

    44→def _low_subjective_dimensions(

    48→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    49→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:

    60→                    strict_val,

    62→                )
    63→            )
    64→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])

     ### Subjective review
     Run `desloppify review --prepare` first to generate review data, then use Claude subagents:
     1. **Prepare**: `desloppify review --prepare`
     2. **Launch subagents**: Split the review across N parallel Claude subagents

Implements a staged workflow: OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT
with a fast-track CONFIRM-EXISTING skip path.

Stage gates validate **plan data enrichment**, not text reports:
- OBSERVE: lightweight — write an analysis of themes/root causes (100 char min)
- REFLECT: compare current findings against completed work (recurring patterns)
- ORGANIZE: structural — each manual cluster must have description + action_steps
- COMMIT: strategy must be substantive (200 char min) or "same"
"""

def _short_id(fid: str) -> str:
    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a finding ID for compact display.

    Finding IDs look like ``review::.::holistic::dim::identifier::abcdef12``.
    Commands accept the hash suffix as a shorthand for the full ID.
    """
    if "::" in fid:
        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
        if len(suffix) >= 8:
            return suffix[:8]
    return fid
